Motivation: 晶格手术是容错量子计算的主流路线，将逻辑线路编译为晶格手术操作决定了计算资源开销。已有编译器基于理想化假设：或不可扩展，或在层内逐门路由，导致嵌入依赖门的顺序且丧失一次路由多门的机会；同时假设魔法态始终可用且零成本，而实际制备一个魔法态比 Clifford 操作更昂贵。因此，面向实际场景的编译器尚待开发。

Method: FlowRouter 将 Clifford 路由与随机魔法态培养置于同一个 3D 嵌入中。编译期，把整个线路层的路由建模为最大流问题，实现多门同时嵌入；运行期，允许在每个空闲 patch 上培养魔法态，兼容带复用与多级后选择的真实培养过程，并引入延迟机制以尽可能小的时空体积代价吸收随机性。

Result: 在假设魔法态免费时，FlowRouter 相比最优静态拓扑晶格手术编译器减少 2.3 倍时空体积，编译速度快 10.8 倍；在同时计入 Clifford 与魔法态成本的培养感知设置下，于距离-13 表面码上相比最优运行时编译器减少 2.3 倍时空体积。

Conclusion: FlowRouter 是首个在实际设定下工作的拓扑晶格手术编译器，通过层内最大流同时路由与空闲 patch 培养魔法态的联合优化，显著降低时空体积并提升编译速度，为容错量子计算的资源开销优化提供了实用化方案。

Abstract: Lattice surgery on the surface code is a leading route to fault-tolerant quantum computation. Compiling logical circuits into lattice surgery operations is a pivotal step that determines the resource cost of a computation. Prior compilers build on idealized assumptions, and one that works in practical settings remains to be developed. Existing works are either unscalable or route one gate at a time within a layer, so the embedding depends on the order of gates and opportunities to route many gates at once are lost. In addition, they assume magic states are always available and cost-free, even though preparing one costs more than a Clifford operation.
  We present FlowRouter, the first topological lattice surgery compiler that places Clifford routing and stochastic magic state cultivation inside a single 3D embedding. At compile time, FlowRouter formulates the routing of an entire circuit layer as a maximum-flow problem, embedding many gates simultaneously. At runtime, it allows magic states to be cultivated at every idling patch, works with the realistic cultivation process with multiplexing and multi-stage post-selections, and absorbs the stochasticity by introducing delay mechanisms that add as little spacetime volume as possible. With magic states assumed free, FlowRouter reduces spacetime volume by 2.3x and compiles 10.8x faster than the state-of-the-art static topological lattice surgery compiler. With the cultivation-aware setting that includes both Clifford and magic state cost, FlowRouter reduces spacetime volume by 2.3x on a distance-13 surface code when compared with the state-of-the-art runtime compiler.

</details>


### [100] [A four-state quantum communication protocol with mesoscopic twin beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23818)
*Stefano Carsi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将基于双光束(TWB)态的量子通信协议从二进制编码扩展到四态方案，每轮传输两比特，并利用NRF实现状态判别与窃听检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TWB态具有强光子数关联，其非经典性可由噪声降低因子(NRF)量化，既可作为量子关联的判据，也可用于信道安全监测。原方案仅支持二进制编码，传输容量有限，因此需要扩展为多态编码以提升每轮信息量并保持安全性。

Method: 通过组合两个平均光子数取值与双光束单臂上热噪声的两个模式数，在(m,R)平面定义四个可区分状态以实现两比特编码：平均探测光子数m用于恢复第一比特，NRF用于区分第二比特并提供安全校验。开展两轮实验：先表征TWB源的光子数统计、NRF与探测效率以确定信道工作条件；随后实施四态协议，分析状态分布及其95%置信区域、错误概率随样本量的变化，并比较机器学习分类器的状态判别效果；最后考察NRF对拦截-重发攻击的响应。

Result: 在介观区利用SiPM等光子数分辨探测器直接验证了TWB态的光子数关联；四态协议的状态分布具有可分离的95%置信区域，错误概率随样本量下降，多种机器学习分类器可用于状态判别；拦截-重发攻击引入额外噪声并退化量子关联，导致NRF发生可观测变化。

Conclusion: 该工作将TWB量子通信协议从二进制扩展至四态，实现每轮两比特传输，并证明NRF既能判别第二比特又能作为窃听检测的安全指标，为介观区基于光子数关联的安全量子通信提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Twin-beam (TWB) states generated by parametric down-conversion exhibit strong photon-number correlations that can be exploited for quantum communication. In the mesoscopic regime, these correlations can be directly investigated using photon-number-resolving detectors, such as silicon photomultipliers (SiPMs). Their nonclassical nature is quantified by the noise reduction factor (NRF), which provides a witness of quantum correlations and a means of monitoring channel security. In this work, a quantum communication protocol based on TWB states, originally proposed for binary encoding, is extended to a four-state scheme capable of transmitting two bits per use. Information is encoded by combining two mean photon-number values and two mode numbers of thermal noise superimposed on one arm of the twin beam, defining four distinguishable states in the (m,R) plane. While the mean detected photon number enables the retrieval of one bit, the NRF provides discrimination of the second bit and a security check against intercept-and-resend attacks, which introduce additional noise and degrade the quantum correlations. The protocol is investigated through two experimental measurement campaigns. First, the twin-beam source is characterized in terms of photon-number statistics, NRF, and detection efficiency to establish the operating conditions of the communication channel. Subsequently, the four-state protocol is implemented and evaluated through the analysis of state distributions and their 95% confidence regions, error probability as a function of sample size, and a comparative assessment of machine learning classifiers for state discrimination. Finally, the response of the NRF to an intercept-and-resend attack is investigated to assess the protocol's ability to detect eavesdropping.

</details>


### [101] [Hidden collision statistics in bosonic heat transport: Superthermal correlations at fixed mean current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23870)
*Iu. A. Nosal,A. E. Teretenkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 相同平均弛豫率的库可通过能量输运平均值无法区分，但在涨落层面存在差异。本文研究玻色模式与冷热热辅助系统的泊松流经有限分束器碰撞的耦合，揭示了平均演化、稳态结构、热流矩及超热聚束规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有相同平均弛豫率的库在平均能量输运层面不可区分，然而会产生不同的涨落。为了刻画这种平均量相同但高阶统计不同的物理机制，作者研究有限强度碰撞型热库对玻色模式的动力学影响，区分平均行为与涨落行为。

Method: 将玻色模式通过有限分束器碰撞与冷、热热辅助系统的泊松流耦合。平均演化为由有限高斯事件信道生成的复合泊松半群；非平衡稳态为受随机仿射不动点支配的热态精确混合。通过Fock空间对角化和蒙特卡洛轨迹方法进行数值验证，并分析事件分辨两点测量记录下热量累积量的渐近速率。

Result: 非平衡稳态是热态的精确混合，由随机仿射不动点控制。平均占据数动力学和分库平均热流与匹配的连续Lindblad库一致，但高阶关联保留碰撞强度信息。当碰撞透射率相等时，得到由温度对比度和碰撞强度控制的精确超热聚束定律。获得前两个热累积量的渐近速率，并分离出局域单碰撞贡献与时间关联贡献。Fock空间对角化和蒙特卡洛轨迹验证了公式，并将完全重置与弱碰撞高斯极限联系起来。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，平均弛豫率相同的热库虽在平均能量输运上等价，但有限碰撞强度会在高阶涨落中留下印记。所建立的复合泊松半群与随机仿射不动点框架，为区分统计等价库、理解非平衡稳态涨落及超热聚束提供了精确理论工具。

Abstract: Reservoirs with the same mean relaxation rate can be indistinguishable at the level of average energy transport while producing different fluctuations. We study a bosonic mode coupled to hot and cold Poisson streams of thermal ancillas through finite beam-splitter collisions. The averaged evolution is a compound-Poisson semigroup generated by finite Gaussian event channels. Its nonequilibrium steady state is an exact mixture of thermal states governed by a random affine fixed point. Consequently, the mean-occupation dynamics and bath-resolved mean heat currents coincide with those of the matched continuous Lindblad reservoir, whereas higher correlations retain the collision strength. For equal collision transmissivities, we derive an exact superthermal bunching law controlled by the temperature contrast and collision strength. We also obtain the asymptotic rates of the first two heat cumulants for an ideal stationary event-resolved two-point-measurement record and separate local one-collision contributions from temporal correlations. Fock-space diagonalization and Monte Carlo trajectories validate the formulas and connect full resets to the weak-collision Gaussian limit.

</details>


### [102] [Bell-certified retardance polarimetry from CHSH correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23914)
*J. Sumaya-Martinez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明Bell-CHSH测量的符合计数数据不仅可作为非局域性见证，还可为光学延迟量相位传感提供定量的度量学证书，并给出无需量子态层析的保守置信下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bell-CHSH测量通常仅被用作非局域性判据，其数据中蕴含的度量学信息未被充分利用；作者希望让标准Bell测试数据集同时给出非局域性基准与统计可控的灵敏度证书，用于量子偏振测量。

Method: 针对编码在单光子上的局域双折射相位，从实验可得的CHSH关联量直接推导经典Fisher信息，并给出用测量关联及CHSH参数对相位导数表示的下界；分析无偏边缘两量子比特区域中规范CHSH分析设置的同时Bell最优与Fisher最优性；进一步考虑可见度下降、相位噪声、分析器失准和有限符合计数，构建无需量子态层析即可评估的保守置信下界。

Result: 在理想情况下，规范CHSH设置可饱和用于延迟量估计的量子Fisher信息；在非理想条件下得到保守的置信下界；并表明非零Fisher信息可在CHSH违背阈值以下持续存在，说明Bell违背与度量学有用性是不同的资源。

Conclusion: 标准Bell测试数据可同时提供非局域性基准和统计受控的灵敏度证书，从而拓展了偏振纠缠光子CHSH测量在量子偏振测量中的用途。

Abstract: Bell-CHSH measurements with polarization-entangled photons are commonly used as nonlocality witnesses. Here we show that the same coincidence data can also provide a quantitative metrological certificate for optical retardance sensing. For a local birefringent phase encoded on one photon, we derive the classical Fisher information directly from experimentally accessible CHSH correlators and obtain a lower bound expressed in terms of the measured correlations and the phase derivative of the CHSH parameter. In the unbiased-marginal two-qubit regime, the canonical CHSH analyzer settings are shown to be simultaneously Bell-optimal and Fisher-optimal, saturating the quantum Fisher information for retardance estimation in the ideal case. We further analyze reduced visibility, phase noise, analyzer misalignment, and finite coincidence counts, and construct conservative lower confidence bounds that can be evaluated without quantum-state tomography. The results also clarify that Bell violation and metrological usefulness are distinct resources: nonzero Fisher information can persist below the CHSH-violation threshold. The protocol therefore enables a standard Bell-test data set to provide both a nonlocality benchmark and a statistically controlled sensitivity certificate for quantum polarimetry.

</details>


### [103] [Trace-distance-based complementarity relations in a multipath interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24074)
*Yue Sun,Jingyan Liu,Peng-Tong Li,Chenxu Li,Ming-Jing Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用迹距离（trace distance）量化多路径干涉仪中的波动性与粒子性，并据此建立若干波粒二象性与"三象性"关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 干涉仪中的互补关系是量子力学波粒二象性的重要体现，但如何在多路径情形下同时量化波动与粒子行为仍值得深入。

Method: 将迹距离作为波动性与粒子性的度量候选，推广至多路径干涉仪，并据此推导互补关系。

Result: 发现迹距离是波动与粒子度量的合适候选，分别建立了若干二象性关系与三象性关系。

Conclusion: 该工作拓展了迹距离在干涉仪中的应用，并为波动性与粒子性的量化提供了新视角。

Abstract: The complementarity relations in an interferometer reflect an important phenomenon in quantum mechanics: wave-particle duality. Here, we develop a method to quantify both wave and particle behaviors in a multi-path interferometer. In particular, we find that the trace distance is a good candidate for wave and particle measures. As a result, some duality relations and triality ralations are established respectively. This work not only extends the application of the trace distance to the interferometer, but also opens up new perspectives on the quantification of waveness and particleness.

</details>


### [104] [Pauli-resolved virtual distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24132)
*Si-Yuan Chen,Congcong Zheng,Kun Wang,Ming-Cheng Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明虚拟蒸馏态 ρ^m/tr(ρ^m) 的全部泡利谱可以在多项式样本复杂度内学习，较此前协议实现系统规模 n 上的指数加速，并提出相干贝尔差分采样电路与量子伯恩斯坦范数来刻画这一加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习虚拟蒸馏量子态 ρ^m/tr(ρ^m) 的完整泡利谱此前需要指数多份 ρ 的副本，样本复杂度是核心瓶颈，作者希望突破这一指数障碍。

Method: 利用成对副本抵消泡利算符的反交换符号，用单个 2m-副本测量设置学习全部 4^n 个平方泡利矩；对 m=2 提出相干贝尔差分采样电路；引入量子伯恩斯坦范数作为非线性泛函的可计算不相容性度量；并给出信息论层面的 m-副本协议恢复带符号矩。

Result: 全部 4^n 个平方泡利矩 [tr(Pρ^m)]^2 可用 O(m[n+log(1/δ)]/ε^2) 份副本达到加性误差 ε、置信度 1-δ，实现系统规模 n 上的指数加速；带符号矩协议样本复杂度为 O(m[n+log(1/δ)]/ε^4)；在给定下界区间内固定 m 时达到最优副本数，更少副本需指数多份 ρ。

Conclusion: 配对副本对泡利反交换符号的抵消是加速根源，量子伯恩斯坦范数为此提供了可计算的不相容性认证；该工作给出了虚拟蒸馏态泡利谱学习的近最优样本与副本复杂度刻画。

Abstract: Learning the full Pauli profile of the virtually distilled quantum state $ρ^m/\text{tr}(ρ^m)$ has so far required exponentially many copies of $ρ$. We show that all $4^n$ squared Pauli moments $[\text{tr}(Pρ^m)]^2$ can be learned to additive error $\varepsilon$ with confidence $1-δ$ from one $2m$-replica measurement setting using $O(m[n+\log(1/δ)]/\varepsilon^2)$ copies. This is an exponential speedup in the system size $n$ over previous protocols. For $m = 2$, we propose the coherent Bell difference sampling circuit that realizes this measurement on current devices. The speedup originates from paired replicas that cancel the anticommutation signs of Pauli operators, collapsing the incompatibility of the Pauli family. We certify this collapse by introducing the quantum Bernstein norm, a computable incompatibility measure for nonlinear functionals. A further information-theoretic $m$-replica protocol recovers the signed moments $\text{tr}(Pρ^m)$ using $O(m[n+\log(1/δ)]/\varepsilon^4)$ copies. For fixed $m$ in the stated lower-bound regime, it attains the optimal replica number, since any protocol with fewer replicas requires exponentially many copies.

</details>


### [105] [Sparse and weak-measurement certification of graph-edge entanglement in PXP scar wavepackets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24139)
*Ximo Wang,Yichi Zhang,Qiwei Han,Xi Zhao,Yuhang Wang,Chunxiao Du,Rui Li,Wenxiu Li,Hao Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种有限记录协议，用于在图修饰的PXP链的疤痕波包上探测可局域化纠缠，通过加入图稳定子探针并施加扰动预算与交换子界，在二十自旋链上认证全部十九条图边，并区分可局域化资源与真正多体认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不完美的多体复现本身并不能证明返回态的纠缠；需要在有限记录、非精确本征态、且哈密顿量持续活跃的条件下，可靠地认证疤痕波包上的可局域化纠缠资源。

Method: 针对图修饰PXP链的疤痕波包设计有限记录协议：目标团簇态具非零能量方差，不假设精确本征态或暗态嵌入；使用未变形哈密顿量泡利项的二元探针（存在完全可分解释），再加入局域图稳定子探针以获得纠缠见证及同时置信界；对指定平方根仪器，用最坏情况扰动预算确定强度以最小化等分配的Hoeffding充分采样成本；并用局域交换子界处理哈密顿量活跃期间的有限时长辅助脉冲。

Result: 在二十自旋以内的链、扰动动力学及独立实现上测试：在首次二十自旋返回处，合成弱记录认证了全部十九条图边；生成元、双色、有界权重和全群基准将可局域化资源与真正多体认证区分开。

Conclusion: 该协议可测量已知疤痕波包中的可恢复纠缠，并给出显式校准及其物理与统计解释的界限。

Abstract: An imperfect many-body revival does not by itself certify the entanglement of the returning state. We give a finite-record protocol for graph-edge localizable entanglement along scar wavepackets of a graph-dressed PXP chain. The target cluster state has nonzero energy variance, so neither an exact target eigenstate nor a dark-state embedding is assumed. Fresh binary probes of the undeformed Hamiltonians Pauli terms have a fully separable explanation, even within the dressed blockade sector. Adding local graph-stabilizer probes makes established entanglement witnesses accessible with simultaneous confidence bounds. For a specified square-root instrument, an imposed worst-case disturbance budget fixes the strength that minimizes the equal-allocation Hoeffding sufficient sampling cost. A local commutator bound accounts for finite-duration ancilla pulses while the Hamiltonian remains active. We test the protocol on chains through twenty spins, under perturbed dynamics, and with independent implementations. At the first twenty-spin return, synthetic weak records certify all nineteen graph edges. Generator, two-color, bounded-weight, and full-group benchmarks separate this localizable resource from genuine multipartite certification. The protocol measures recoverable entanglement in a known scar wavepacket, with explicit calibration and limits on its physical and statistical interpretation.

</details>


### [106] [Reconstructing the phonon distribution of trapped ions out of the Lamb-Dicke regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24147)
*Christophe H. Valahu,Prachi Nagpal,Teerawat Chalermpusitarak,Maverick J. Millican,Cameron McGarry,Frank Scuccimarra,Vassili G. Matsos,Hon-Kwan Chan,Ting Rei Tan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将囚禁离子声子态重构转化为滤波函数反演问题，利用载波与多边带的复合脉冲设计在声子空间良态的滤波器，突破Lamb-Dicke区限制，实现纯态/混态表征、加热动力学研究及n=250的Fock态重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 囚禁离子运动态的声子分布表征对量子信息处理至关重要，但现有方法在超出Lamb-Dicke区后条件数变差（ill-conditioned），难以可靠重构。

Method: 将声子态重构重新表述为滤波函数反演问题；在载波和多个边带上施加复合脉冲，从而在声子空间中工程化出条件数良好的滤波器。

Result: 可对纯态与混态进行表征，研究加热动力学，并重构高达n=250的Fock态。

Conclusion: 基于滤波函数反演的复合脉冲方案克服了传统方法在Lamb-Dicke区外的病态问题，将囚禁离子声子态重构拓展至大声子数区间。

Abstract: Characterizing the phonon distribution of trapped-ion motional states is essential for many applications in quantum information processing, but existing methods become ill-conditioned beyond the Lamb-Dicke regime. We overcome this limitation by recasting phonon-state reconstruction as a filter-function inversion problem. Using composite pulses on the carrier and multiple sidebands, we engineer well-conditioned filters in phonon space to characterize pure and mixed states, study heating dynamics, and reconstruct Fock states up to $n=250$.

</details>


### [107] [Nondemolition filtering of an embedded cluster-state scar under continuous local monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24166)
*Ximo Wang,Xi Zhao,Xiayu Sun,Qiwei Han,Yuhang Wang,Chunxiao Du,Wenxiu Li,Hao Zhang,Rui Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造含精确簇态本征矢的局域环状哈密顿量，在连续监测下研究稳定子缺陷动力学，给出有限时间保真度保证与资源限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别多体谱中的低纠缠本征态与在测量中保持它，是两个不同的任务；需要明确测量假设、有限时间保证与资源限制的实例化构造。

Method: 构造具有精确簇态本征矢的局域环状哈密顿量；对稳定子缺陷进行连续监测分析；用缺陷算符正间隙推导有限时间界；通过精确对角化（至11量子比特）、独立矩阵与轨迹计算以及保守相干误差界进行验证。

Result: 任意混合输入下的条件簇保真度等于初始目标权重除以未观测到点击的概率；正缺陷算符间隙给出的有限时间界适用于非对易哈密顿动力学、非正规有效生成元和不完美探测；固定总监测速率下，保证指数随系统尺寸反比下降；高条件保真度不能消除由初始重叠决定的制备成本；数值证据显示在混沌谱背景中存在簇态离群点。

Conclusion: 该构造具体化了已有的疤痕嵌入与非破坏性验证框架，并明确给出了测量假设、有限时间保证与资源限制。

Abstract: Identifying a low-entanglement eigenstate inside a many-body spectrum and preserving it during measurement are distinct tasks. We construct an explicit local ring Hamiltonian with an exact cluster-state eigenvector and study continuous monitoring of its stabilizer defects. For arbitrary mixed inputs, the conditional cluster fidelity is the initial target weight divided by the no-observed-click probability. A positive defect-operator gap gives finite-time bounds that hold for noncommuting Hamiltonian dynamics, nonnormal effective generators and imperfect detection. At fixed total monitoring rate, the guaranteed exponent falls inversely with system size; high conditional fidelity does not remove the preparation cost set by the initial overlap. Exact diagonalization up to eleven qubits gives finite-size evidence for a cluster-state outlier in a chaotic spectral background. Independent matrix and trajectory calculations verify the dynamics and a conservative coherent-error bound. This construction specializes established scar embedding and nondemolition verification frameworks, with explicit measurement assumptions, finite-time guarantees and resource limitations.

</details>


### [108] [No violation on a generalisation of Leggett-Garg inequality and Bell-CHSH inequality with extended probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24169)
*Sirawit Kajonsombat,Pongwit Srisangyingcharoen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用扩展概率替代经典概率概念，在一致历史框架下推广了LGI与Bell-CHSH不等式，得到在任意测量设置下均满足而不被违反的广义不等式，并引入广义宏观实在论与广义局域实在论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典LGI与Bell-CHSH不等式在量子力学中常被违反，其根源在于建立在经典概率假设之上。作者希望借助扩展概率这一更深层的实在层面，重新界定不等式边界，从而在无需依赖具体测量的情况下理解量子系统为何能表现出经典性质。

Method: 将经典概率替换为扩展概率，使其作为经典概率之下的底层实在；在一致历史框架中，该底层对应"不可定居的历史"（non-settleable history）。据此推导LGI与Bell-CHSH不等式的扩展边界，并在扩展概率框架内引入并分析广义宏观实在论与广义局域实在论。

Result: 所得到的广义不等式在任意测量设置下均被满足、不发生违反；广义宏观实在论与广义局域实在论可在不依赖显式测量的条件下被定义与分析。

Conclusion: 通过扩展概率与一致历史的结合，可为量子系统兼具经典性质提供新的理论解释路径，为后续研究开辟了可能性。

Abstract: A generalisation of the Leggett-Garg inequality (LGI) and the Bell-CHSH inequality is proposed in this work, by replacing a classical probability notion with an extended probability notion. The extended probability serves as an underlying layer of reality to the classical probability. Within the consistent history framework, the underlying layer is so-called a non-settleable history. Hence, the bounds of the LGIs and Bell-CHSH inequalities are extended. The resulting generalised inequalities are satisfied without violation for arbitrary measurement settings. Furthermore, generalised Macro- realism and generalised Local realism are introduced and analysed within the extended probability framework without relying on an explicit measurement. This opens up further possibilities on one of the great pursuits in physics, understanding on how a quantum system can possess classical properties.

</details>


### [109] [A QSVT-Based Quantum Jacobi Algorithm for Linear Systems with Application to the Poisson Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24266)
*Louisa M. Piskol,Thorsten Grahs,Stefan Langer,Oleksandr Kyriienko*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子奇异值变换(QSVT)的Jacobi迭代量子实现，将Jacobi迭代重构为分块编码算子的多项式变换，辅助比特数恒定、电路深度随迭代次数线性增长，并在1D/2D Poisson问题及方腔驱动流的压力Poisson方程上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD中的离散化会产生大规模稀疏线性方程组。经典上多用迭代法求解，但现有量子线性求解器多基于矩阵求逆来获得解，而非通过迭代过程逼近解，与CFD领域成熟的迭代求解范式不匹配。作者希望在量子框架下复现Jacobi这类经典迭代方法，为后续量子多重网格和预处理技术奠定基础。

Method: 采用量子奇异值变换(QSVT)实现Jacobi方法。核心思想是把Jacobi迭代重新表述为对分块编码(block-encoded)算子的多项式变换，从而以量子线路形式执行迭代。该构造在迭代次数上仅需常数个辅助比特，同时电路深度随迭代次数线性增长。

Result: 在一维和二维Poisson问题上演示了算法，并进一步应用于Chorin投影法中出现的压力Poisson方程(方腔驱动流,lid-driven cavity flow)。结果表明该算法在辅助比特开销和电路深度上具有前述的可控标度特性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了量子Jacobi迭代求解器，可为未来量子多重网格方法与预处理技术提供有前景的构建模块，推动量子算法向CFD成熟求解策略靠拢。

Abstract: Many computational fluid dynamics (CFD) algorithms solve partial differential equations by discretization, resulting in large and sparse systems of linear equations. While iterative methods are widely used to solve these systems classically, most existing quantum linear system solvers target the solution through matrix inversion rather than approximating it using an iterative procedure. In this work, we develop a quantum implementation of the Jacobi method based on quantum singular value transformation (QSVT). By reformulating the Jacobi iteration as a polynomial transformation of a block-encoded operator, the algorithm requires only a constant ancilla overhead with respect to the number of iterations while maintaining a circuit depth that scales linearly with the iteration number. We demonstrate the algorithm for one- and two-dimensional Poisson problems, including the pressure Poisson equation arising in Chorin's projection method for the lid-driven cavity flow. The proposed algorithm provides a promising building block for future quantum implementations of multigrid methods and preconditioning techniques, bringing quantum algorithms closer to established CFD solution strategies.

</details>


### [110] [On the Born rule in a new quantum approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24269)
*Inge S. Helland*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子基础的新研究路径下，通过若干步骤，在一个弱条件下证明了玻恩规则；该唯一弱条件称为"跃迁概率原理"，与统计学的似然原理密切相关，并对该条件从多个角度进行了讨论，最终得到一个比有限维量子力学传统概念更简单的态概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻恩规则是量子力学的核心概率假设，传统上多作为公设引入。作者希望在新量子基础研究框架下，以尽可能弱的假设重新论证玻恩规则，从而简化量子力学的公理基础与态概念。

Method: 以"跃迁概率原理"作为唯一弱条件，将其与统计学的似然原理相关联，并通过若干逻辑步骤逐步推导玻恩规则；随后从多个视角对该条件进行讨论与辩护。

Result: 在仅依赖跃迁概率原理这一弱条件的情况下，成功推导出玻恩规则；该条件与似然原理具有紧密联系，并被多角度加以阐释，同时由此得到一个比有限维量子力学传统态概念更简单的态概念。

Conclusion: 玻恩规则可在弱于传统公设的条件下被证明，其基础可归结为跃迁概率原理；该路径不仅简化了量子基础的假设结构，也带来了更简洁的态概念。

Abstract: In the context of a new approach towards quantum foundation, the Born rule is proved under a weak condition through several steps. The sole weak condition behind the Born rule is called the transition probability principle, which is closely related to the likelihood principle of statistics. This condition is discussed from several points of view. The whole approach leads to a simpler state concept than the traditional one in finite-dimensional quantum mechanics.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum Computing Solution of the Bethe-Salpeter Equation for Relativistic Scalar Bound States via Tensor-Network VQE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24282)
*Gerhard Hellstern*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于量子门线路求解齐次Bethe-Salpeter方程（hBSE）的方法，将问题化为N=2^n维对称矩阵本征值问题，用VQE结合MPS张量网络拟态求解；在N=16时最低耦合常数本征值复现精度优于1%，但纠缠分析与三重障碍评估表明该特定问题仍处于经典可处理区域，属负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子计算能否为相对论束缚态问题（hBSE）带来优势。作者希望在量子比特编码框架下建立hBSE的求解范式，并量化其纠缠特性，从而判断该问题是否真正适合量子加速。

Method: 对两质量标量粒子在梯近似标量交换下的hBSE做Wick旋转至欧氏空间并作O(4)S波分波投影，约化为维度N=2^n的对称矩阵本征值问题；将哈密顿量分解为n量子比特Pauli算符之和，用MPS张量网络拟态的变分量子本征求解器（VQE）求解，并与经典对角化对比，同时分析纠缠度与VQE梯度行为。

Result: 在N=16（n=4量子比特）下，VQE对编码最小束缚耦合常数的最大本征值复现平均相对误差优于1%。BSE振幅呈现低纠缠、近似面积律特性，支持MPS拟态。三重障碍分析显示：Pauli开销指数增长、纠缠随规模近似不变且偏低、VQE梯度随尺寸下降（与贫瘠高原担忧一致），表明该问题在测试范围内由低键维张量网络良好描述，可被MPS/Lanczos高效处理。

Conclusion: 该负面结果首次给出hBSE振幅在量子比特编码下的纠缠量化，建立了Pauli哈密顿量框架以供未来BSE变体使用；并指出二维Minkowski空间BSE、N体束缚态以及非梯近似核等物理动机明确的扩展方向，可能成为真正量子优势的候选场景。

Abstract: We present a gate-based quantum computing solution of the homogeneous Bethe-Salpeter equation (hBSE) for the bound state of two massive relativistic scalar particles interacting via ladder-approximation scalar exchange. After Wick rotation to Euclidean space and O(4) S-wave partial-wave projection, the hBSE is reduced to a symmetric matrix eigenvalue problem of dimension N = 2^n. We decompose the resulting Hamiltonian into a sum of n-qubit Pauli operators and solve it with the Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) using a Matrix Product State (MPS) tensor-network ansatz. For N=16 (n=4 qubits), the VQE recovers the maximum eigenvalue - which encodes the minimum coupling constant for binding - to better than 1 % mean relative error compared to classical diagonalization. Entanglement analysis of the BSE amplitude shows low, area-law-like entanglement over the tested sizes, which motivates the MPS ansatz. A critical analysis of three independent barriers - exponential Pauli overhead, approximately size-independent low entanglement, and decreasing VQE gradient scales for the tested ansatz, consistent with generic barren-plateau concerns at larger n - reveals that the specific problem studied here lies in a classically tractable regime: over the tested range it is well described by low-bond-dimension tensor networks and efficiently handled by MPS/Lanczos methods. This negative result provides, to our knowledge, the first entanglement quantification of the BSE amplitude in qubit encoding, establishes the Pauli Hamiltonian framework for future BSE variants, and identifies 2D Minkowski-space BSE, N-body bound states, and non-ladder kernels as physically motivated extensions where genuine quantum advantage may become plausible.

</details>


### [112] [New lower bounds for CDS and $f$-routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24291)
*Atsuya Hasegawa,Ranitha Mataraarachchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究非局域量子计算（NLQC）中$f$-路由的纠缠代价下界这一核心开放问题，建立了稳健CDS共享随机性代价与稳健$f$-路由纠缠代价两项下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解NLQC的纠缠代价与复杂度理论、密码学、量子引力等领域相关，$f$-路由是其核心特例（源于量子位置验证），而全稳健情形下的纠缠代价下界长期未解。

Method: 其一，借助Allerstorfer等（Quantum 2024）建立的CDS与$f$-路由联系，分析稳健CDS的共享随机性代价；其二，研究单侧完美$f$-路由，利用Asadi、Culf与May（ITCS 2025）方法中低秩矩阵的正定性。

Result: 证明稳健CDS的共享随机性代价下界为确定性SMP通信复杂度的对数（即使通信与私有随机性不受限），且对相等函数该下界是紧的；给出以符号秩表示的纠缠代价一般下界，并导出内积函数在两种单侧完美情形下路由纠缠代价的线性下界，与已知上界匹配。

Conclusion: 两项下界为全稳健$f$-路由的纠缠代价提供了新工具与进展，CDS随机性复杂度是通向该问题下界的自然一步，符号秩方法可给出紧致结果。

Abstract: Understanding the entanglement cost of non-local quantum computation (NLQC) is relevant to complexity theory, cryptography, quantum gravity, and related areas. A central special case is $f$-routing, motivated in part by quantum position verification. Proving lower bounds on its entanglement cost in the fully robust setting has been a major open problem in NLQC.
  Motivated by this problem, we establish two related lower bounds. First, we study the shared-randomness cost of robust conditional disclosure of secrets (CDS). The connection between CDS and $f$-routing established by Allerstorfer et al. (Quantum 2024) makes understanding the randomness complexity of robust CDS a natural step toward lower bounds for the fully robust routing problem. We show that the shared-randomness cost of robust CDS is lower bounded by the logarithm of deterministic SMP communication complexity, even when communication and private randomness are unrestricted. Our lower bound is tight for the equality function.
  Second, we consider one-sided-perfect $f$-routing, in which the protocol is exact on one input class and has constant error on the other. By exploiting the positivity of the low-rank matrix arising in the method of Asadi, Culf, and May (ITCS 2025), we derive a general lower bound on the entanglement cost in terms of sign rank. In particular, this yields a linear lower bound on the entanglement cost of routing for the inner-product function in both one-sided-perfect settings, matching the known upper bound.

</details>


### [113] [A Sharp Noise Threshold for Shor's Quantum Factoring and Discrete Log Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24316)
*Jin-Yi Cai,Ben Young*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Shor量子因数分解与离散对数算法在量子傅里叶变换中受控旋转门噪声影响下的渐近行为，识别出一个锐利且极小的噪声阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进Cai (2024)及Cai and Young (2025)的结果，明确Shor算法对噪声容忍的临界条件。

Method: 分析噪声对量子傅里叶变换中精确受控旋转门的影响，推导算法在噪声下的渐近行为并确定噪声阈值。

Result: 若噪声水平低于阈值，两种算法在期望多项式时间内成功；若超过阈值，当底层素数属于某一正密度集合时，算法可证明无法在期望多项式时间内求解。

Conclusion: Shor量子因数分解与离散对数算法存在一个锐利且极小的噪声阈值，区分了多项式时间可解与不可解两种情形。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of Shor's quantum factoring and discrete log algorithms when noise affects the precise controlled rotation gates in their quantum Fourier transforms. Improving the results of Cai (2024) and Cai and Young (2025), we identify a sharp and vanishingly small noise threshold. If the noise level lies below this threshold, then the two algorithms succeed in expected polynomial time. If the noise level exceeds this threshold, then the algorithms provably fail to solve their respective problems in expected polynomial time when the underlying primes belong to a set of positive density.

</details>


### [114] [Robust self-testing of nonmaximal entanglement from a reduced inner product game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24349)
*Ranyiliu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将Lalonde伪心灵感应内积博弈从六维降到五维，证明约化博弈能自检验非最大纠缠态及全部35个测量效应，并给出鲁棒性界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lalonde伪心灵感应内积博弈原本定义在六维，其自检验性质维数较高；降低维度可简化自检验结构，并用于刻画哪些纠缠态（非最大纠缠态）能完美赢得该博弈。

Method: 保留Alice四个问题、Bob三个问题，将每个答案字母表缩减到五个，构造五维约化博弈；通过自检验论证证明参考非最大纠缠态与全部35个测量效应被唯一确定，并用局部提取信道下最优平方保真度Ξ定义d_ext来刻画鲁棒性。

Result: 策略以至少1-ε概率获胜时，d_ext ≤ min{1, 2^21 √ε}；借助紧性论证得到所有测量作用于该态在共同局部等距下的同时定性鲁棒性；作为推论，任何有限维最大纠缠态都不能完美赢得该博弈。

Conclusion: 给出五维内积博弈，实现非最大纠缠态和全部测量效应的鲁棒自检验，并排除有限维最大纠缠态完美获胜的可能。

Abstract: We reduce Lalonde's pseudo-telepathy inner product game from six to five dimensions, keeping its four Alice questions and three Bob questions and reducing each answer alphabet to five. We prove that the reduced game self-tests its nonmaximally entangled state and all 35 measurement effects. The self-test is also robust: for a strategy winning with probability at least $1-\varepsilon$ we obtain $d_{\mathrm{ext}}\le\min\{1,2^{21}\sqrt{\varepsilon}\}$, where $d_{\mathrm{ext}}=\sqrt{1-Ξ}$ and $Ξ$ is the optimal squared fidelity with the reference state under local extraction channels. A compactness argument then gives simultaneous qualitative robustness for all measurement actions on the state under common local isometries. As a consequence of the self-test, no maximally entangled state of any finite dimension can win the game perfectly.

</details>


### [115] [Versatile Quantum Machine Learning with an Ultra-low Power Photonic Quantum Reservoir Computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24381)
*Wei Wang,Zan Tang,Menglong Fang,Daiqin Su,Mile Gu,Jayne Thompson,Lip Ket Chin,Hong Cai,Leong-Chuan Kwek,Ai-Qun Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种集成光子量子储备池计算芯片，无需对储备池核心进行主动调谐，即可实现非线性映射、衰减记忆与多功能任务；在零偏置（所有片上移相器断电）状态下仍保持竞争性能，将计算功耗降为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成光子微处理器擅长高带宽、大规模并行线性计算，但实现机器学习所需的非线性特征映射与时间记忆仍是关键挑战。传统方案依赖主动调谐和额外非线性元件，增加了架构复杂度与功耗开销。因此需要一种无需重构内部光网络、低功耗且多功能的硬件范式。

Method: 构建集成光子量子储备池计算机，融合量子态编码、多模干涉混频与光子统计读出。储备池核心无需主动调谐即可产生非线性映射与衰减记忆；同一芯片支持静态分类、动态预测与稳定的自主预测任务，并可在零偏置状态（片上移相器全部不加电）下运行。

Result: 同一芯片在静态分类、动态预测与稳定自主预测任务上均取得准确/稳定表现，且在零偏置状态下保持有竞争力的性能，消除主动控制并将计算功耗降至零。

Conclusion: 该无源运行方式为多功能机器学习硬件提供了可扩展路径：大规模光子量子处理器无需重新配置内部光网络即可复用为储备池。量子态编码、多模干涉混频与光子统计读出的结合，为低功耗、大规模量子储备池计算提供了有物理依据的范式。

Abstract: Integrated photonic microprocessors provide high-bandwidth, massively parallel linear computation, but realizing nonlinear feature maps and temporal memory remain key challenges for machine learning. Conventional approaches rely on active tuning and additional nonlinear elements, increasing architectural complexity and power overhead. Here we demonstrate an integrated photonic quantum reservoir computer that achieves nonlinear mapping, fading memory, and task versatility without active tuning of the reservoir core. The same chip supports accurate static classification, dynamic prediction, and stable autonomous forecasting, establishing broad utility across both classification and temporal inference tasks. Competitive performance is retained in the zero-bias state, where all on-chip phase shifters are unpowered, eliminating active control and reducing computational power consumption to zero. This passive operation highlights a scalable route to multifunctional machine-learning hardware, where large-scale photonic quantum processors can be repurposed as reservoirs without reconfiguring their internal optical networks. By combining quantum-state encoding, multimode interferometric mixing, and photon-statistical readout, this architecture provides a physically grounded paradigm for low-power, large-scale quantum reservoir computing.

</details>


### [116] [Quantum Work Extraction via Conditional Spatial Displacements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24425)
*Necati Çelik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于连续变量指针的测量辅助反馈协议，从相干量子电池中提取功。该协议利用幺正算符使电池能量本征态与指针位置纠缠，再通过指针位置的投影测量条件制备电池纯态，并用反馈幺正操作提取功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子相干性与测量在热力学中的作用，探索如何通过测量辅助反馈从与指针纠缠的量子电池中恢复本会因退相干（追踪指针自由度）而不可提取的功，从而实现量子麦克斯韦妖。

Method: 采用幺正算符 U = exp(-i k t Ĥ ⊗ P̂/ħ) 使电池能量本征态与辅助指针位置产生纠缠；对指针位置进行投影测量以条件制备电池纯态；再用反馈幺正操作提取功。针对两能级量子电池与高斯指针，计算条件态及相应的"妖能"（daemonic ergotropy）。

Result: 对于所考虑的纯初态，发现"妖能"在所有相互作用强度下均等于标准ergotropy，表明测量辅助反馈能够完全恢复因与指针纠缠、在追踪指针自由度时本会失去的可提取功。

Conclusion: 该协议为量子麦克斯韦妖提供了物理上直观的实现，其中指针作为量子测量辅助系统，其位置与电池能量关联；方案适用于囚禁离子系统实验实现，有助于理解量子相干性和测量在热力学中的作用。

Abstract: We propose a protocol for extracting work from a coherent quantum battery state by exploiting measurement-assisted feedback mediated by a continuous-variable pointer. The scheme relies on the unitary operator $U = \exp(-i k t \, \hat{H} \otimes \hat{P}/\hbar)$, which generates entanglement between the battery's energy eigenstates and the position of an auxiliary pointer. A subsequent projective measurement of the pointer's position conditionally prepares the battery in a pure state from which work can be extracted via a feedback unitary. We analyze the protocol for a two-level quantum battery and a Gaussian pointer, computing the conditional states and the corresponding daemonic ergotropy. For the pure initial state considered, we find that the daemonic ergotropy equals the standard ergotropy for all interaction strengths, demonstrating that the measurement-assisted feedback recovers the full extractable work that would otherwise become inaccessible due to entanglement with the pointer when its degrees of freedom are traced out. The protocol thus provides a physically transparent realization of a quantum Maxwell demon, where the pointer acts as a quantum measurement ancilla whose position becomes correlated with the battery's energy. The scheme is amenable to experimental implementation in trapped-ion systems, and it contributes to the ongoing efforts to understand the role of quantum coherence and measurement in thermodynamics.

</details>


### [117] [Subspace Controllability in Variational Quantum Circuits: Maximising Expressivity and Increasing Search Efficiency with Dynamical Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24475)
*Andrew Rowan Barlow,Hans-Martin Rieser,Markus Lange*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究变分混合量子模型在经典机器学习任务中的表现，通过在MNIST-1D多分类任务上使用可控电路，发现取得最低训练和验证损失的量子模型大多具有子空间可控性，表明超参数搜索可限制在此类模型中，从而降低计算成本和时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分混合量子模型是实现量子硬件机器学习的常见范式，但其训练面临变分量子电路损失景观固有的多个难题。已有研究通过扩展子空间可控性概念，提高了量子任务中找到全局最小值的成功率，但这些结果是否适用于经典任务、以及相对超参数搜索是否具有优势仍属未知。

Method: 在MNIST-1D多分类任务中采用可控电路，并与超参数搜索进行对比。

Result: 在超参数搜索中，取得最低训练损失和验证损失的大多数量子模型均为子空间可控模型。

Conclusion: 超参数搜索可以限制在子空间可控模型范围内，从而减少计算成本和时间。

Abstract: Variational hybrid quantum models are a common paradigm for realising machine learning on quantum hardware. Nonetheless, they are challenging to train due to several problems intrinsic to the loss landscapes formed by variational quantum circuits. To mitigate these issues, researchers have extended the idea of subspace controllability leading to an increase in the success rate of variational models in finding the global minimum for quantum-based tasks. However, it remains unknown whether these results extend to classical-based tasks, and if any advantages are realised over a hyperparameter search. We begin to address this question by employing controllable circuits in a multi- classification task using MNIST-1D. Our results show that over a hyperparameter search, the majority of quantum models that achieve the lowest training and validation losses are subspace controllable. These results indicate that the hyperparameter search can be restricted to such models, in turn reducing computational cost and time.

</details>


### [118] [Statistical mechanics of multipartite entanglement in hypergraph states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24508)
*Paolo Scarafile,Giorgia Trotta,Paolo Facchi,Giuseppe Magnifico,Giorgio Parisi,Saverio Pascazio,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文通过统计力学框架研究n量子比特超图态中的多体纠缠，将平均二分纯度映射为2^n个经典二值自旋的有效哈密顿量，并借助精确枚举与小规模之外的模拟退火/并行回火算法，定量预测最大多体纠缠态（MMES）数量与纠缠分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体纠缠的量化与结构刻画通常受限于态空间指数增长，尤其在受限态族中缺乏有效工具；作者希望借助统计力学方法为超图态族中的多体纠缠提供可计算、可推广的分析框架。

Method: 建立纯n量子比特超图态与经典二值自旋构型的对应：每个超图态对应一个自旋构型，平均二分纯度映射为2^n自旋的有效哈密顿量，温度作为插值参数。小系统（n≤5）进行精确枚举，完整表征能量景观与热力学可观测量；大系统（n=6,7）采用模拟退火与并行回火在指数态空间中采样。

Result: 高温对应随机超图态的均匀系综，零温对应MMES；零温熵的指数直接给出超图态集合内MMES的数量。n≤5的精确枚举验证了已知MMES计数；n=6,7给出MMES数量的定量预测，并揭示纠缠在超图态集合中的统计分布规律。

Conclusion: 超图态可作为通过热力学方法研究多体纠缠的理想平台，既提供计算上的进展，也为受限量子态族中纠缠结构的物理理解提供新视角。

Abstract: We investigate multipartite entanglement in a particular family of pure $n$-qubit hypergraph states through a statistical-mechanics framework, where the average bipartite purity maps onto an effective Hamiltonian of $2^n$ classical binary spins. In this correspondence, each hypergraph state uniquely corresponds to a classical spin configuration, while temperature serves as a control parameter that continuously interpolates between a uniform ensemble of random hypergraph states at high temperature and maximally multipartite entangled states (MMES) at zero temperature. Remarkably, the exponential of the zero-temperature entropy directly gives the number of MMES within the set of hypergraph states. For small system sizes ($n \leq 5$), we perform an exact enumeration, fully characterizing the energy landscape and associated thermodynamic observables, and validating known MMES counts. For larger systems ($n = 6$ and $7$), where exact methods become computationally infeasible, we employ simulated annealing and parallel tempering algorithms to efficiently sample the exponentially large state space. Our analysis yields quantitative predictions of the number of MMES and reveals how entanglement is statistically distributed across the sets of hypergraph states. These results establish hypergraph states as an ideal platform for investigating multipartite entanglement through thermodynamic methods, offering both computational advances and physical insights into the structure of quantum entanglement in restricted families of quantum states.

</details>


### [119] [A Reconfigurable Multilayer Quantum Key Distribution Network over Existing Metropolitan Fibre](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24561)
*Mariella Minder,Andreas Siakolas,Elizabeth Pasatembou,Stylianos Mavrikos,Stephanos Yerolatsitis,Konstantinos Katzis,Kyriacos Kalli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文演示了一个部署在城域既有光纤上的七节点多层QKD网络，通过光谱管理、可重构光交换与网状密钥管理层，在无额外发射机条件下扩展物理连通与服务可达性，73天生成119.4 Gbit密钥材料，链路可用率99.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络规模化受限于光纤与设备资源，且在"棕地"部署中须围绕既有经典电信基础设施与QKD技术能力进行工程化设计，因此需要兼顾继承约束的组网架构。

Method: 在既有城域光纤上部署七节点多层QKD网络：比较光谱管理技术以适配各层继承约束；将可信节点环与经可重构光交换互联的三节点子网结合；并在网状密钥管理层上将端点请求映射到可信中继路径，为L1、L3及一次性密码本应用供钥。

Result: 73天内八种链路配置共生成了119.4 Gbit密钥材料，链路可用率达99.1%；随机化端到端请求揭示了密钥消耗、存储密钥状态与量子层重配置之间存在间接的跨层耦合。

Conclusion: 通过可重构性、光谱复用与逻辑密钥管理抽象实现资源共用，可在不增加发射机的情况下扩展物理连通性与服务可达性，为棕地量子网络部署提供可行范式。

Abstract: Scaling quantum networks requires architectures extending end-to-end service reachability despite constrained fibre and equipment. Critically, in brownfield deployments, the quantum network must be engineered around classical telecommunications infrastructure and the operating capabilities of quantum key distribution (QKD) technology. To address this, we demonstrate a seven-node, multilayer QKD network deployed over existing metropolitan fibre. The implementation compares spectral management techniques to accommodate inherited constraints across its layers. It combines a trusted-node ring with a three-node subnetwork interconnected through reconfigurable optical switching, extending physical connectivity and service reachability without an additional transmitter. A meshed key management layer maps endpoint requests onto trusted-relay paths and supplies Layer 1, Layer 3, and one-time-pad applications. Over 73 days, eight link configurations generated 119.4 Gbit of secret key material with 99.1% link availability. Randomised end-to-end requests revealed indirect cross-layer coupling between key consumption, stored-key state and quantum-layer reconfiguration, demonstrating resource sharing through reconfigurability, spectral multiplexing and logical key-management abstraction.

</details>


### [120] [An Exact Operator Formulation of Statistical Quasiparticle Theory for Nonlinear System-Bath Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24603)
*Yu Su,Yao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将耗散子（dissipaton）理论从线性耦合推广到非线性系统-浴耦合，在热场浴空间中把耗散子显式构造为集体算符，并借助递归广义Wick定理自动生成非微扰、非马尔可夫的层级运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Gaussian influence functional方法仅适用于系统与浴的线性耦合，难以处理非线性系统-浴相互作用。作者希望为 dissipaton algebra 提供微观算符基础，并统一线性与非线性耦合的处理框架。

Method: 针对Gaussian浴，在热场浴空间中将统计准粒子（耗散子）显式构造为集体算符；通过递归广义Wick定理纳入非线性相互作用，直接从Liouville方程生成层级运动方程，实现对多项式耦合的自动方程生成。

Result: 构建了耗散子代数的微观算符基，能够对多项式耦合自动生成层级方程，并把显式浴算符表示与非微扰、非马尔可夫动力学联系起来。

Conclusion: 该框架将线性与非线性系统-浴相互作用统一处理，为开放量子系统的统计准粒子理论提供了超出标准Gaussian influence functional设定的算符表述。

Abstract: We develop an operator formulation of statistical quasiparticle theory for open quantum systems with nonlinear system-bath coupling, extending beyond the linear coupling setting of the standard Gaussian influence functional formulation. For Gaussian baths, the statistical quasiparticles, termed dissipatons, are explicitly constructed as collective operators in the thermofield bath space. Our construction provides a microscopic operator basis for the dissipaton algebra and generates hierarchical equations of motion directly from the Liouville equation. Nonlinear interactions are incorporated through a recursive generalized Wick's theorem, enabling automatic generation of hierarchical equations for polynomial couplings. The resulting framework connects explicit bath operator representations to nonperturbative, non-Markovian dynamics and unifies the treatment of linear and nonlinear system-bath interactions.

</details>


### [121] [Explicit closed-form propagators for time-dependent gain-loss master equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24617)
*Léonce Dupays*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文针对含时速率主方程中的时间排序难题，推导了生成元$\mathcal{L}(t)=\alpha(t)A+\beta(t)B$在特定对易关系下的时序与非时序传播子的显式闭合因子分解，并应用于增益-损耗Lindblad方程获得精确动力学映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含时速率驱动开放系统动力学中的时间排序（时序/非时序传播子）是核心障碍。已有方法如Wei-Norman分解需要求解耦合非线性微分方程组，求解困难。作者希望找到一种无需解非线性微分方程、直接确定因子分解系数的构造方法。

Method: 设生成元为$\mathcal{L}(t)=\alpha(t)A+\beta(t)B$，且对易子$[A,B]$是$A$、$B$伴随作用的共同本征算符，即$[A,[A,B]]=\lambda_A[A,B]$、$[B,[A,B]]=\lambda_B[A,B]$。在此条件下构造时序与反时序传播子的显式闭合因子分解，并直接确定因子分解系数，避免求解耦合非线性微分方程。

Result: 该方法应用于玻色增益-损耗Lindblad方程，得到任意含时增益和损耗速率下的精确动力学映射。推导了初始Fock态Wigner函数的时间演化，并与独立的相空间结果进行对比验证。进一步用所得动力学映射评估了二项式玻色编码在驱动增益-损耗动力学下的编码空间存活概率。

Conclusion: 所提出的因子分解构造为含时驱动开放系统提供了精确、闭合形式的动力学映射，无需数值求解非线性微分方程，并在玻色增益-损耗模型及量子纠错编码存活概率分析中得到验证与应用。

Abstract: Time ordering is a central obstacle in driven open-system dynamics governed by master equations with time-dependent rates. We derive explicit closed-form factorizations of the chronological and antichronological propagators for a time-dependent generator of the form $\mathcal{L}(t)=α(t)A+β(t)B$, when the commutator $[A,B]$ is a simultaneous eigenoperator of the adjoint actions of $A$ and $B$, namely $[A,[A,B]]=λ_{A}[A,B]$ and $[B,[A,B]]=λ_{B}[A,B]$. In contrast to the disentangling procedure known as the Wei-Norman decomposition, our construction determines the factorization coefficients directly, without solving a coupled system of nonlinear differential equations. Applying this result to the bosonic gain-loss Lindblad equation, we obtain the exact dynamical map for arbitrary time-dependent gain and loss rates. The gain-loss master equation provides a canonical description of a driven harmonic oscillator coupled to a thermal bath. We benchmark the construction by deriving the time evolution of the Wigner function of an initial Fock state and comparing it with independent phase-space results. We further apply the resulting dynamical map to evaluate the code-space survival probability of a binomial bosonic code under driven gain-loss dynamics.

</details>


### [122] [Phase space anatomy of dynamical quantum phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24636)
*Zakaria Mzaouali*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出离散相空间中，动力学量子相变的两类定义（序参量转变与回归率非解析性）因所需信息尺度不同而本质分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学量子相变通常有两种定义：晚期序参量行为的转变，或量子态回归率（return rate）的非解析性。作者试图厘清这两者是否为同一物理现象，特别是在离散相空间框架下。

Method: 采用离散相空间（quasiprobability/negativity）框架，比较局部约化态与全局回归所需信息；对 stabilizer 回归推导回归率的精确分解，将其分为失去支撑（loss of support）与破坏性量子干涉两类代价；并以 qutrit Potts 链动力学与一个精确可控模型作为具体算例。

Result: 序参量转变仅由局部约化态决定，无需准概率负性；而一般全局回归可包含任何真约化态都不具备的信息。qutrit Potts 链显示选择性相消可逆转竞争性块回归的排序并延迟其交换；精确控制模型展示出在交点处负性为零的热力学回归尖点（cusp）。

Conclusion: 回归奇异性的存在与干涉在选择其分支中的作用是两个不同的物理问题；离散相空间在信息尺度上根本区分了序参量转变与回归率非解析性这两类动力学量子相变定义。

Abstract: Dynamical quantum phase transitions (DQPT) are commonly defined either by the behavior of a late-time order parameter or by nonanalyticities in a quantum state's return rate. We show that discrete phase space fundamentally separates these two notions by the scale of information they require. An order parameter transition is determined by a local reduced state, requiring no quasiprobability negativity. In contrast, general global returns can contain information absent from every proper reduced state. For stabilizer returns, we derive an exact decomposition of the rate into distinct costs from loss of support and destructive quantum interference. Dynamics of a qutrit Potts chain show that selective cancellation can reverse the ranking of competing block returns and delay their exchange. An exact control also exhibits a thermodynamic return cusp with zero negativity at the crossing. The existence of a return singularity and the role of interference in selecting its branches are therefore distinct physical questions.

</details>


### [123] [Circuit Hypernetworks for Quantum-Augmented Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24657)
*Xiaoqiang Wang,Mengyang Xiong,Jun Dai,Bang Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: HyperQ在冻结的掩码扩散语言模型上添加token条件化的量子残差分支，仅训练分支，通过精确经典期望值实现16–64量子比特电路的线性成本训练，平均分数从47.65提升至54.30。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子电路用于语言模型适配时，在大型模型内部求值较大规模电路计算代价高，因此需要一种可扩展且易训练的量子增强架构。

Method: 在冻结的掩码扩散语言模型每个Transformer块中加入量子残差分支：分支读取token隐状态，由轻量级电路超网络在该token上生成共享稀疏电路结构中的旋转角、耦合强度与测量轴，执行电路并将测量值经残差连接加回；主干冻结，仅训练新增分支。所需期望值具有精确经典表达式，其评估成本随量子比特数线性增长。

Result: 在11亿参数主干中可训练16至64量子比特电路；随电路宽度增加，下游基准平均分由47.65升至54.30；64量子比特时较主干及其低秩适配版本分别高4.71和3.67分；HyperQ仅用2万条提示-响应对微调，而经典基线用20万条。

Conclusion: token条件化的电路生成是一种可行的量子增强语言建模架构方法。

Abstract: Language models can be adapted by changing the computations applied to individual tokens. Quantum circuits offer one such approach, but evaluating wider circuits inside a large model can be computationally demanding. Here we introduce HyperQ, which adds token-conditioned quantum residual branches to a frozen masked-diffusion language model. A quantum residual branch is a module in each transformer block that reads a token's hidden state, emits the coordinates of that token's circuit, executes it, and adds the measured values back through a residual connection. The backbone remains frozen, and only the added branches are trained. Within each branch, a lightweight circuit hypernetwork emits token-specific rotation angles, coupling strengths, and measurement axes in a shared sparse circuit structure. The required expectation values have an exact classical expression whose evaluation cost grows linearly with the qubit count, enabling circuits from 16 to 64 qubits to be trained within a 1.1-billion-parameter backbone. Across downstream benchmarks, increasing circuit width raises the average score from 47.65 to 54.30. At 64 qubits, HyperQ exceeds the backbone and its low-rank-adapted counterpart by 4.71 and 3.67 points, respectively. HyperQ is fine-tuned on 20,000 prompt-response pairs, compared with 200,000 for the classical baselines. These findings support token-conditioned circuit emission as a tractable architectural approach to quantum-augmented language modelling.

</details>


### [124] [Experimental evidence of generalization in quantum machine learning in small-data regime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24666)
*Leena Anthony,Artemiy Burov,Nicolas Piro,Matteo Dal Peraro,Clément Javerzac*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了一个硬件兼容、含中期测量与经典前馈的QCNN，验证其在极少样本下可有效学习，并揭示其扩展瓶颈主要来自数据编码与硬件执行而非优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像、临床试验与罕见病等领域面临数据稀缺这一核心瓶颈，量子机器学习被视为小样本学习的潜在范式。QCNN因层级结构、强归纳偏置及参数量随系统规模仅对数增长而备受关注，其吸引力源自Caro等人（2022）的泛化界：泛化误差随可训练参数量而非希尔伯特空间维度增长，使其可能处于样本高效区间。作者希望验证该理论优势在真实硬件上是否可实现，并厘清其实际扩展限制。

Method: 作者设计了一个硬件兼容的QCNN，采用中期测量（mid-circuit measurement）与经典前馈机制。在二分类手写数字任务上，从仅10个训练样本起步，随训练集增大观察泛化误差变化，并与参数规模匹配（45参数）的经典卷积网络及约25,000参数的无约束经典基线对比。随后，他们将振幅编码与角度编码电路在2×2至512×512像素的图像分辨率间进行转译，分析两种编码方案的规模扩展特性。最后在医学动机的BreastMNIST基准上进行验证。

Result: 在二分类手写数字任务上，QCNN仅用10个训练样本即取得较强测试性能，且泛化误差随训练集扩大而下降；在45参数的匹配预算下，QCNN能够学习，而同等规模的经典卷积网络停留在随机水平；但在数据充足时，约25,000参数的无约束经典基线仍表现最强。转译实验揭示了主导扩展瓶颈：振幅编码保持量子比特高效但电路深度急剧增长，角度编码保持浅层但所需量子比特数变得不可接受。在BreastMNIST上，QCNN未超过无约束经典网络，但以少数个数量级的参数稳定地高于随机水平学习。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对QCNN而言，从极少样本中学习在实践中是可行的；而向真实图像数据扩展时，约束更多来自数据编码与硬件执行，而非优化过程。

Abstract: Quantum machine learning is a promising paradigm for learning from limited data, a central bottleneck in domains such as medical imaging, clinical trials, and rare diseases. Quantum convolutional neural networks (QCNNs) are particularly attractive in this setting, combining a hierarchical architecture with strong inductive bias and a parameter count that grows only logarithmically with system size. Their appeal rests on the generalization bounds of Caro et al. (2022), which show that the generalization error of a quantum model scales with the number of trainable parameters rather than with the Hilbert-space dimension, placing QCNNs in a potentially sample-efficient regime. We develop a hardware-compatible QCNN with mid-circuit measurement and classical feed-forward, and show on a binary handwritten-digit task that strong test performance is achievable from as few as 10 training samples, with the generalization error decreasing as the training set grows. At a matched 45-parameter budget the QCNN learns where an equally small classical convolutional network stays at chance, although an unconstrained classical baseline with roughly 25,000 parameters remains strongest when data are plentiful. Transpiling amplitude and angle encoded circuits across image resolutions from 2x2 to 512x512 pixels then exposes the dominant scaling bottleneck: amplitude encoding stays qubit-efficient but grows extremely deep, whereas angle encoding stays shallow but becomes qubit-prohibitive. On the medically motivated BreastMNIST benchmark the QCNN does not surpass the unconstrained classical network, yet it learns consistently above chance using orders of magnitude fewer parameters. Our results indicate that for QCNNs, learning from few samples is attainable in practice, whereas scaling to realistic image data is constrained less by optimization than by data encoding and hardware execution.

</details>


### [125] [Wigner-positive quantum states can have lower entropy than the vacuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24670)
*Zacharie Van Herstraeten,Nicolas J. Cerf,Ulysse Chabaud*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过构造解析反例否定了Wigner熵猜想及其更强的Wigner大化猜想，反例违反熵下界的幅度不超过10^-3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wigner熵猜想认为纯高斯态使非负Wigner函数的Shannon熵最小。检验并挑战这一猜想（及更强的Wigner大化猜想）对理解量子态的非高斯性与不确定性原理的相互作用具有重要意义。

Method: 将一个特定的纯Wigner负态与另一个适当选取的量子态混合，使Wigner函数恢复非负性，同时保持Wigner熵严格略低于真空熵；基于极端非负Wigner函数的刻画构造解析反例，并分析其物理机制。

Result: 得到违反熵下界不超过10^-3的简单解析反例，从而否定Wigner熵猜想与Wigner大化猜想；此类亚真空Wigner熵态可任意接近真空，且在0<α<2时呈现亚真空Wigner-Rényi α-熵。

Conclusion: Wigner熵猜想及其更强的Wigner大化猜想被证伪；亚真空Wigner熵源于不确定性原理与非高斯性之间的微妙相互作用，此类态可任意接近真空。

Abstract: The Wigner entropy conjecture posits that pure Gaussian states minimize the Shannon entropy of non-negative Wigner functions, known as the Wigner entropy. We show that mixing a specific pure Wigner-negative state with another suitably chosen quantum state can restore Wigner positivity while retaining a Wigner entropy that is slightly - but strictly - below the vacuum entropy. As a consequence, we disprove the Wigner entropy conjecture, as well as the stronger Wigner majorization conjecture, by deriving simple analytical counterexamples with violations of the entropy bound no larger than $10^{-3}$. Such states with sub-vacuum Wigner entropy can be found arbitrarily close to the vacuum and can also exhibit sub-vacuum Wigner-Rényi $α$-entropy for $0<α<2$. We identify the physical mechanism behind the existence of such states, which arises from a subtle interplay between the uncertainty principle and non-Gaussianity. The key idea of this paper was developed with the help of AI tools, building on the characterization of extreme non-negative Wigner functions.

</details>


### [126] [Unified spatiotemporal quantum states and spatiotemporal entanglement from Kirkwood-Dirac phase space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24680)
*Zhian Jia,Mei-Hui Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用时空Kirkwood-Dirac相空间，构建了时空量子态的统一表征框架，通过(准)概率混合涵盖现有表述，并推广至s参数化形式、OTOC、KMS条件及时空纠缠分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子态概念局限于空间域，而时空量子态虽将量子态推广到时空域（用单位迹算符编码时空量子事件关联），但已有表述零散、缺乏统一框架。本文旨在建立统一的时空量子态表征理论。

Method: 采用时空Kirkwood-Dirac相空间刻画时空量子态；通过准概率混合统一各类表述；推导时空态的递归表达式；将构造推广至多重关联函数并联系OTOC；建立基于s参数化时空态(准)概率混合的更一般框架，并引入Petz时间反演；最后应用熵度量分析时空纠缠。

Result: 发现Kirkwood-Dirac分布导出的时空态一般为非厄米、非正规，而Margenau-Hill分布导出的为厄米；通过(准)概率混合统一了几乎所有现有表述；建立了量子非经典性与时空态时间性间的关系；将多重关联函数与OTOC关联；给出Petz时间反演及两时间KMS条件的应用；并用互补熵度量刻画了时空纠缠。

Conclusion: 时空Kirwood-Dirac相空间为时空量子态提供了统一且可推广的表征框架，衔接了非经典性、时间性、OTOC、KMS条件与时空纠缠等多个概念，为时空量子信息研究奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The notion of a spatiotemporal quantum state extends the conventional concept of a spatial quantum state to the spatiotemporal domain. Such states are represented by unit-trace operators that encode correlations among quantum events distributed across space and time. In this work, we use the spatiotemporal Kirkwood-Dirac phase space to provide a unified characterization of spatiotemporal quantum states. Spatiotemporal states obtained from Kirkwood-Dirac distributions are generally non-Hermitian and nonnormal; whereas those constructed from Margenau-Hill distributions are Hermitian. We provide a unification via (quasi)probabilistic mixture of Kirkwwod-Dirac spatiotemporal states which encompasses almost all existing formulations of spatiotemporal quantum states. We derive recursive expressions for spatiotemporal states and elucidate the relation between Kirkwood-Dirac nonclassicality and the temporality of spatiotemporal states. We further extend the construction to many-fold correlation functions and establish its connection with out-of-time-ordered correlators (OTOCs). We also develop a more general unifying framework based on (quasi)probabilistic mixture of $s$-parametrized spatiotemporal states and establish their Petz time reversal and application in studying Kubo-Martin-Schwinger (KMS) condition in two-time setting. Finally, we apply this framework to characterize quantum entanglement in spacetime and analyze spatiotemporal entanglement using several complementary entropy measures.

</details>


### [127] [Superconducting qubit based on altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24759)
*Xue-Feng Pan,Xin-Lei Hei,Franco Nori,Peng-Bo Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用超导体-交错磁体-超导体结（SAS结）的可编程约瑟夫森势，提出了一类新型类transmon量子比特，兼具大非谐性与抗退相干能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体具有零净磁化与动量依赖的自旋劈裂，为下一代约瑟夫森器件提供了新平台；同时，高性能超导量子比特需要同时具备高相干性、大非谐性与可调性，现有方案难以兼顾。

Method: 在SAS结中研究约瑟夫森效应，通过器件设计工程化预设的电流-相位关系；基于交错磁性实现的可编程约瑟夫森势，构建类transmon超导量子比特，并在2φ结区分析其双库珀对相干隧穿机制及电荷噪声、磁通噪声抑制特性。

Result: 在2φ结区，该量子比特因宇称保护而具备对电荷噪声的固有抗性；磁通可精确调控量子比特，且在适当偏置下进一步抑制电荷噪声与磁通噪声。

Conclusion: 交错磁体可作为约瑟夫森势工程的多功能平台，为兼具高相干性、大非谐性与宽可调性的高性能超导量子比特开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Altermagnets, characterized by vanishing net magnetization and momentum-dependent spin splitting, provide a promising platform for next-generation Josephson devices. Here, we exploit the Josephson effect in superconductor-altermagnet-superconductor junctions and show how to engineer prescribed current-phase relations by device design. Based on these programmable Josephson potentials utilizing altermagnetism, we propose a new class of transmon-like superconducting qubits that combine large anharmonicity with enhanced robustness against decoherence via coherent two-Cooper-pair tunneling. We show that in the $2φ$-junction regime, this kind of qubit provides intrinsic protection against charge noise due to parity protection. Magnetic flux can be used to precisely control the qubit and, under appropriate bias, this architecture further suppresses charge and flux noise. Our results establish altermagnets as a versatile platform for Josephson-potential engineering and open a new route toward high-performance superconducting qubits combining high coherence, large anharmonicity, and broad tunability.

</details>


### [128] [Observable-targeted variational quantum simulation of Hamiltonian dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24794)
*Leonardo Zambrano,Luciano Pereira,Antonio Acín*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种面向目标观测量的变分量子模拟方法，仅更新电路参数以复现选定期望值的演化，在相同测量预算下显著延长容错模拟时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准变分量子模拟旨在复现完整量子态的演化，但许多实际应用仅需少数观测量的期望值，复现全态造成不必要的测量与资源开销。

Method: 研究纯态哈密顿量的变分动力学方法，通过更新电路参数以复现选定期望值的演化；利用精确误差恒等式指导观测量的选择，并基于目标量与哈密顿量的重复对易子构造可观测量；对泡利可观测量与泡利旋转电路，无需辅助量子比特或受控操作即可估计参数更新。

Result: 在六比特自旋、费米子和分子基准测试中，与标准变分量子模拟相比，在相同测量次数预算下，目标化更新将目标误差容限内的中位时间最多延长约4.2倍。

Conclusion: 将变分更新定向到目标可观测量可在不增加每时间步测量成本的前提下延长精确模拟的有效时间。

Abstract: Standard variational quantum simulation seeks to reproduce the evolution of the full quantum state, although many applications require only the expectation values of a few observables. We study a variational method for pure-state Hamiltonian dynamics that updates circuit parameters to reproduce the evolution of selected expectation values. An exact error identity guides the choice of observables, motivating a construction based on repeated commutators of the target with the Hamiltonian. For Pauli observables and Pauli-rotation circuits, the update can be estimated without ancillary qubits or controlled operations for overlap estimation. Across six-qubit spin, fermionic, and molecular benchmarks, the targeted update extends the median time within the target-error tolerance by up to a factor of $4.2$ relative to standard variational quantum simulation at equal shot budgets. These results show that directing the variational update toward the target observable can extend accurate simulation without increasing the measurement cost per time step.

</details>


### [129] [Stabilizing temporal quantum-enhanced sensitivity via sub-optimal measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24805)
*Wangsheng Zheng,Yaoling Yang,Abolfazl Bayat*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了多参量非平衡量子传感的普适时标定标律，揭示次优测量下精度呈受有界振荡调制的二次标定，并提出分组分时测量协议以消除振荡、稳定鲁棒二次标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子增强灵敏度通常以精度随时间的超线性标定为核心资源，但其实现依赖最优测量；最优测量往往复杂且含时，实践中难以实现，而次优测量通常无法保持稳健的超线性标定并出现振荡。因此需要研究次优测量下的普适精度行为，并寻找可行方案恢复稳健的超线性（二次）标定。

Method: 首先在通用多参量非平衡量子传感框架下建立普适时标定标律，刻画次优测量下传感精度的普适行为；随后提出分组测量协议，将测量划分为不同组并分别在不同时刻执行，通过集合精度消除振荡项；并以三个不同示例及贝叶斯估计进行验证。

Result: 在次优测量下，传感精度普遍表现为受有界振荡函数调制的二次标定；所提出的分组分时测量协议能够在多参量情形下、即使使用次优测量也稳定获得鲁棒的二次标定；该结论通过三个具体例子与贝叶斯估计得到验证。

Conclusion: 该协议适用于任何有信息的测量，为量子增强灵敏度的实际实现迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: In non-equilibrium probes, quantum-enhanced sensitivity is quantified through super-linear scaling of precision with respect to time, as a central metrology resource. However, this requires optimal measurements, which are often complex and time-dependent, making it challenging in practice. Sub-optimal measurements typically fail to retain robust super-linear scaling and show oscillations. Here, we begin by establishing a universal temporal scaling law in a general multi-parameter non-equilibrium quantum sensing framework. Within this setting, we identify the universal behavior of the sensing precision with sub-optimal measurements which shows quadratic scaling modulated by an additional bounded oscillatory function. To remove the oscillatory part, we propose a protocol in which measurements are partitioned into different groups, each performed at different times. The collective precision obtained from this protocol stabilizes a robust quadratic scaling even for sub-optimal measurements in the multi-parameter regime. We validate our protocol through three distinct examples as well as Bayesian estimation. Our protocol is applicable to every informative measurement and takes a key step towards practical realization of quantum-enhanced sensitivity.

</details>


### [130] [Trade-offs and experimental feasibility of nonlocal polygamy with two-outcome Bell inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24835)
*Josep Batle,Tomasz Rybotycki,Tomasz Białecki,Piotr Gawron,Adam Bednorz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究多体量子系统中Bell不等式的同时违反问题，识别可在IBM Quantum硬件上演示的构型，并推导多种"多夫"（polygamous）构型的界限与权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠与Bell非局域性在多体系统各子系统间具有不同的共享约束，理解这些约束的极限需要同时考察多方Bell不等式违反行为，并将理论界限与真实量子硬件的实验能力相对应。

Method: 考察每方采用二结果可观测量、每方2或3个测量设置时的Bell不等式同时违反；筛选可在IBM Quantum硬件上实现的构型，并对若干polygamous构型推导界限与权衡关系。

Result: 识别出可在IBM Quantum硬件上演示同时违反的配置，并给出多个polygamous构型的定量界限与权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子计算机的实验能力与刻画量子关联极限的数学方法相连接，为多方非局域性共享约束的研究提供了理论与实验结合的路径。

Abstract: Entanglement and Bell nonlocality have different sharing constraints across subsystems of a multipartite quantum system. We examine simultaneous violations of Bell inequalities with two-outcome observables and two or three measurement settings per party. We identify configurations whose simultaneous violations can be demonstrated on IBM Quantum hardware, and derive bounds and trade-off relations for several polygamous configurations. The results connect experimental capabilities of quantum computers with mathematical methods for studying the limits of quantum correlations.

</details>


### [131] [Overcoming the Hong-Ou-Mandel interference limitation for the second photon from a two-photon cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24858)
*Raphael Joos,Michal Vyvlecka,Tim Strobel,Benjamin Breiholz,Furkan Aglarci,Ponraj Vijayan,Hans-Georg Babin,Arne Ludwig,Michael Jetter,Simone L. Portalupi,Peter Michler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文表明：级联三能级系统发射的纠缠光子对中，来自第二跃迁的光子其双光子干涉极限仅源于干涉分束器处时间重叠下降；通过四光子符合测量与后置同步，可恢复最大干涉可见度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体量子点等量子发射器可经级联态发射纠缠光子对，但三能级阶梯系统中两光子间的时间关联会对可实现的双光子干涉构成限制，需要厘清该限制的物理来源并寻找突破途径。

Method: 进行理论与实验研究，测量两个先后发射的光子对之间的时间分辨四光子符合；并采用后置发射同步手段考察干涉可见度的变化。

Result: 对于第二跃迁发射的光子，其干涉受限仅由干涉分束器处时间重叠降低所致；后置发射同步后，干涉可见度恢复至最大值。

Conclusion: 三能级级联系统的时间关联并非不可克服的根本限制，而可通过后置同步加以消除；在未来量子网络中，该同步可由确定性量子存储器实现。

Abstract: Quantum emitters such as semiconductor quantum dots can provide entangled photon pairs emitting from a system of cascaded states. However, the resulting temporal correlation between the two photons from the three-level ladder system has been shown to set a limit on the achievable two-photon interference. In this work, we show that such limitation, for photons emitted by the second transition, is only due to reduced temporal overlap at the interference beam splitter. We investigate this theoretically and experimentally by determining time-resolved four-photon coincidences between two successively emitted photon pairs. Post-emission synchronization shows the recovery of maximum interference visibility; in prospective quantum networks this could be achieved via deterministic quantum memories.

</details>


### [132] [Explanation of the Observed Energy Exchange through the vacuum in Optomechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24900)
*Vincenzo Macrì,Franco Nori,Alessandro Ferreri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出现有腔光力学大失谐近似忽略高阶镜-场相互作用，无法解释Fong等观测到的真空介导的膜间传热。作者用全量子模型证明被忽略的高阶光力作用自然产生声子-声子耦合，可定量解释实验中的能量交换，并提出可提取净功的声子-声子驱动循环协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准腔光力学在大失谐区仅保留辐射压项、忽略高阶镜-场相互作用，该近似无法从微观上解释Fong等[Nature 576, 243 (2019)]实验中机械膜之间真空介导的热传导，其物理起源仍存争议，需给出微观解释并突破线性近似。

Method: 建立全量子模型，纳入此前被忽略的高阶光力相互作用项。

Result: 高阶光力相互作用自然产生声子-声子耦合，能在标准光力框架内定量复现实验观测的能量交换。

Conclusion: 确立高阶光力相互作用的根本地位，为超出线性近似的下一代光力学实验提供微观理论框架，并提出声子-声子相互作用驱动的可提取净功的循环协议。

Abstract: In cavity optomechanics, the standard large-detuning regime is commonly described by retaining only the radiation-pressure interaction, while higher-order mirror--field interactions are assumed to be negligible. This approximation, however, fails to provide a microscopic explanation for the vacuum-mediated heat transfer observed between the mechanical membranes in the experiment of Fong, et.al.,[Nature \textbf{576}, 243 (2019)], whose physical origin has remained under active debate. Here, using a fully quantum model, we show that the neglected higher-order optomechanical interactions naturally generate phonon-phonon coupling and quantitatively account for the observed energy exchange within the standard optomechanical framework. Building on this microscopic description, we further propose a protocol in which phonon-phonon interaction drives a cyclic process enabling net work extraction. Our results establish the fundamental role of higher-order optomechanical interactions and provide a microscopic framework for describing next-generation optomechanical experiments beyond the linear approximation.

</details>


### [133] [A thermal microwave bus for neutral atom quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24933)
*Matthew J. H. Kendall,Christopher J. Watson,Michael Ben Shem,Jonathan D. Breeze*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出四能级架构，利用微波腔耦合里德伯态实现长程门，并引入双色拉曼门抑制衰减，最终将腔介导门用于环面码和双变量自行车码以加速纠错轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子阵列中的高保真双比特门依赖里德伯阻塞，但该机制本质上是短程的，长程连接需要通过原子穿梭（shuttling）实现，这带来额外开销与误差。因此需要一种既能保持基态量子比特长相干寿命、又能实现长程连接的门方案。

Method: 提出四能级架构：基态量子比特保持长寿命，里德伯态与微波腔耦合以实现腔介导的长程门。首先分析两种既有协议（Tavis-Cummings模型中的色散iSWAP、驱动腔产生的受控相位门）的热依赖性，并在里德伯衰减、热光子、有限腔线宽下进行模拟，给出保真度及闭式边界。随后提出双色拉曼门，仅虚拟布居里德伯态并抵消色散位移，以抑制原子与腔衰减。最后在全光镊阵列置于微波腔中，用腔介导门闭合环面码的周期边界。

Result: 双色拉曼门达到保真度 F = 0.997。在现实阵列尺寸下，用腔介导门闭合环面码周期边界可将纠错轮次缩短 2.3 倍；应用于双变量自行车码族时，可将gross code的一轮缩短 4.8 倍。

Conclusion: 通过微波腔介导的长程门结合双色拉曼协议，能够在中性原子阵列中实现高保真长程连接，并显著缩短量子纠错轮次，为环面码及双变量自行车码等量子LDPC码的实用化提供路径。

Abstract: High-fidelity two-qubit gates in neutral-atom arrays rely on the Rydberg blockade, which is intrinsically short ranged and requires long range connectivity to be achieved through atom shuttling. We propose a four level architecture, where the typical ground state qubit can be leveraged for its long lifetime and the Rydberg states couple to a microwave cavity, allowing for long range cavity mediated gates. We first find the thermal dependence of two established protocols, the dispersive iSWAP native to the Tavis-Cummings model and the Controlled phase gate generated from driving the cavity. We simulate them under the presence of Rydberg decay, thermal photons, finite cavity linewidth and find fidelities which accompany closed form bounds. We then introduce the bichromatic Raman gate, which only virtually populates the Rydberg states and cancels dispersive shifts to mitigate both atomic and cavity decay, achieving a fidelity of $F = 0.997$. Finally, we consider a full optical tweezer array in a microwave cavity, and show that using a cavity mediated gate to close the periodic boundaries of the toric code shortens an error correction round by a factor of 2.3 for realistic array sizes. This was then applied to the wider family of Bivariate bicycle codes, and we find that it shortens a round of the gross code by a factor of 4.8.

</details>


### [134] [Efficient Heralding of Loss-Tolerant Photonic GHZ States for Device-Independent Conference Key Agreement over Long Distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24941)
*Yazeed K. Alwehaibi,Makoto Ishihara,Shakib Daryanoosh,Ewan Mer,Shang Yu,Wojciech Roga,Ian A. Walmsley,Masahiro Takeoka,Raj B. Patel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究损耗量子网络中预报式多体纠缠分发与设备无关会议密钥协商（DI-CKA），揭示GHZ态的光子数编码方式对损耗容忍度的关键影响，提出基于异质源的星型网络协议，直接预报真空-n光子GHZ态，实现对现有探测器可行的DI-CKA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备无关会议密钥协商（DI-CKA）在损耗量子网络中的实现依赖预报式多体纠缠分发。虽然GHZ态在无损耗时局部等价，但损耗存在时不同单轨光子数编码的损耗容忍度差异显著，而此前考虑的固定光子数GHZ态所需探测效率较高，难以用现有探测器实现，因此需要寻找更优编码与源架构。

Method: 1）比较计算基GHZ态（真空与n光子分量的相干叠加）与固定光子数GHZ态，推导两类态临界探测效率的精确解析条件；2）提出使用异质源的星型网络协议，直接预报真空-n光子GHZ态，其长距离标度为O(η_c^{n/2})；3）针对四用户情形，刻画光子损耗下的预报态，并论证可调源参数能在任意有限信道距离下保持真正的多体纠缠；4）分别考虑理想Pauli测量与实验可行的位移测量，并分析基于SPDC的实现及源致不对称对宇称-CHSH测量角色分配的影响。

Result: 理论证明计算基GHZ态可在显著更低的探测效率下实现无探测漏洞的宇称-CHSH违背；星型异质源协议通过可调源参数在任意有限信道距离下保持真正的多体纠缠；在理想Pauli测量和位移测量下均能以现有光电探测器可达到的探测效率实现DI-CKA，同时密钥率与通信距离与既有预报方案相当；SPDC实现分析表明源致不对称使宇称-CHSH测试中的测量角色分配变得关键。

Conclusion: 光子数编码、源架构与测量角色分配是设计损耗容忍多体量子网络并提升DI-CKA性能的关键设计参数，所提协议使DI-CKA在现有探测器条件下可行。

Abstract: Heralded multipartite entanglement distribution is a key requirement for device-independent conference key agreement (DI-CKA) over lossy quantum networks. Although locally equivalent in the absence of loss, different single-rail photon-number encodings of Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states can exhibit substantially different loss tolerance. We show that computational-basis GHZ states, comprising a coherent superposition of vacuum and an $n$-photon component, enable detection-loophole-free parity-CHSH violations at markedly lower detection efficiencies than previously considered fixed-photon-number GHZ states, and derive exact analytical conditions for the critical detection efficiencies of both state classes. Motivated by this advantage, we introduce a star-network protocol using heterogeneous sources to directly herald vacuum-$n$-photon GHZ states with long-distance scaling $O(η_{\mathrm{c}}^{n/2})$, where $η_{\mathrm{c}}$ is the channel transmittance. For four users, we characterize the heralded state under photon loss and show that tunable source parameters preserve genuine multipartite entanglement at any finite channel distance. For both ideal Pauli measurements and experimentally accessible displacement-based measurements, our protocol enables DI-CKA at detection efficiencies achievable with current photodetectors, while retaining key rates and communication distances comparable to those of previous heralded schemes. We discuss physical implementations and analyze an SPDC-based realization, showing that source-induced asymmetry can make measurement-role assignment in the parity-CHSH test crucial. These results identify photon-number encoding, source architecture, and measurement-role assignment as design parameters for loss-tolerant multipartite quantum networks and enhanced DI-CKA performance.

</details>


### [135] [Tight entropy contraction beyond detailed balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24953)
*Li Gao,Jingyu Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文超越细致平衡条件，为有限维量子信道建立定量熵收缩界：对与忠实条件期望相容的信道，证明GNS范数收缩可导出相对熵收缩，仅损失维度常数的对数因子；对具有忠实渐近条件期望的量子马尔可夫半群，给出完全修正对数Sobolev常数以GNS谱隙表示的下界，且允许初始与环境纠缠、界不依赖环境系统尺寸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典与量子熵收缩研究多依赖细致平衡条件，限制了适用范围。本文旨在突破该限制，为更一般的有限维量子信道与量子马尔可夫半群建立定量熵收缩估计，并允许初始态与外部环境存在纠缠，从而扩展熵收缩理论的适用性与紧致性。

Method: 针对与忠实条件期望相容的信道，利用GNS（Gelfand–Naimark–Segal）范数收缩推导相对熵收缩，并控制维度常数仅产生对数损失；对具有忠实渐近条件期望的量子马尔可夫半群，通过GNS谱隙给出完全修正对数Sobolev常数的下界；分析中允许初始态与环境纠缠但界不依赖环境尺寸；并应用于交替储层脉冲与连续耦合能量阶梯模型。

Result: 建立了超越细致平衡的量化熵收缩界：GNS范数收缩蕴含相对熵收缩，维度代价仅为对数因子；得到完全修正对数Sobolev常数基于GNS谱隙的下界；界对初始环境纠缠稳健且与环境系统尺寸无关；在交替储层脉冲与连续耦合能量阶梯应用中给出具有最优尺寸依赖性的界。

Conclusion: GNS范数收缩可作为超越细致平衡条件下相对熵收缩的有效工具，并在量子马尔可夫半群中联系到完全修正对数Sobolev常数与GNS谱隙。所得界适用于含环境纠缠的情形且尺寸依赖性最优，为储层脉冲与能量阶梯等物理模型提供紧致估计。

Abstract: We establish quantitative entropy contraction bounds for finite-dimensional quantum channels beyond detailed balance. For channels compatible with a faithful conditional expectation, we show that GNS (Gelfand--Naimark--Segal) norm contraction yields relative entropy contraction with only a logarithmic loss in the dimensional constant. For quantum Markov semigroups with a faithful asymptotic conditional expectation, this gives a lower bound on the complete modified logarithmic Sobolev constant in terms of the GNS spectral gap. The bounds allow initial entanglement with an external environment and are independent of its system size. Applications to alternating reservoir pulses and continuously coupled energy ladders give bounds with optimal size dependence.

</details>
